  "short_description": {
    "id": "Questing Beast atau Beast Glatisant adalah monster campuran berbagai hewan dalam banyak teks legenda Arthurian abad pertengahan, yang diburu sia-sia oleh Raja Pellinore.",
    "en": "The Questing Beast or Beast Glatisant is a composite-animal monster of many medieval Arthurian texts, vainly hunted by King Pellinore.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "questing-beast-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Questing Beast, atau Beast Glatisant (Prancis Kuno/Old French: beste glatisant; Prancis Modern/Modern French: bête glatissante), adalah monster campuran hewan yang muncul dalam banyak teks abad pertengahan legenda Arthurian dan karya modern yang terilhami olehnya. Dalam siklus prosa Prancis (French) dan Le Morte d'Arthur, perburuannya menjadi pencarian sia-sia Raja Pellinore (King Pellinore) dan keluarganya, yang akhirnya dituntaskan Sir Palamedes dan kawan-kawannya. Namanya berasal dari bunyi keras yang keluar dari perutnya; glatisant berkerabat dengan kata Prancis glapissant, \"menyalak\".",
      "en": "Called the Questing Beast or the Beast Glatisant (Old French beste glatisant, today's French bête glatissante), this monster pieced together from several animals turns up in many medieval Arthurian texts and in modern works drawing on them. In the French prose cycles and Le Morte d'Arthur, chasing it is the fruitless quest of King Pellinore and his kin, at last fulfilled by Sir Palamedes and his companions. Its name comes from the great noise issuing from its belly; glatisant is related to French glapissant, 'yelping'.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "questing-beast-c01",
        "questing-beast-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Para sarjana Arthurian cenderung menafsirkannya sebagai cerminan pandangan abad pertengahan tentang jerapah, yang nama genus Camelopardalis berasal dari gambaran setengah unta setengah macan tutul. Dalam Post-Vulgate, Merlin mengungkapkan bahwa makhluk itu lahir dari seorang putri yang bernafsu kepada saudaranya sendiri; sebelum mati dicabik anjing, saudaranya menubuatkan bahwa ia akan melahirkan makhluk keji yang bersuara seperti kawanan anjing itu. Menurut artikel de, ia berkepala ular, bertubuh macan tutul, berpantat singa, dan berkaki serta berkuku rusa, dan dari dalamnya terdengar gonggongan seperti tiga puluh anjing yang sedang berburu.",
      "en": "Arthurian scholars tend to read it as reflecting medieval ideas of the giraffe, whose name Camelopardalis came from its description as half camel, half leopard. In the Post-Vulgate, Merlin reveals it was born of a princess who lusted after her own brother; before he was torn apart by dogs, the brother foretold she would bear an abomination sounding like that pack of hounds. According to the de article it has a serpent's head, a leopard's body, a lion's hindquarters and a deer's legs and hooves, and from inside it comes a baying like thirty hounds in full chase.",

      "quote": "is a cross-animal monster appearing in many medieval texts of the Arthurian legend and modern works inspired by them.       "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Questing Beast (Beast Glatisant; Prancis Kuno/Old French beste glatisant; Modern French bête glatissante) monster campuran dalam legenda Arthurian; siklus prosa Prancis (French), Le Morte d'Arthur; diburu Raja Pellinore (King Pellinore), dituntaskan Sir Palamedes; di Britania dan Prancis.",
        "en": "The Questing Beast (Beast Glatisant; Old French beste glatisant; Modern French bête glatissante) is a composite Arthurian monster; in the French prose cycles and Le Morte d'Arthur hunted by King Pellinore, achieved by Sir Palamedes."
      }

    {
      "id": "questing-beast-c02",
      "source_id": "questing-beast-s1",
      "quote": "Its name comes from the great noise that it emits from its belly (such motif seems to have originated in a symbolic dream scene in the Gesta Regum Anglorum). Glatisant is related to the French word glapissant, 'yelping' or 'barking', especially of small dogs or foxes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama dari bunyi keras dari perutnya; glatisant terkait kata Prancis (French) glapissant, menyalak.",
        "en": "Named for the noise from its belly; glatisant relates to French glapissant, yelping."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "questing-beast-c03",
      "source_id": "questing-beast-s1",
      "quote": "Arthurian scholars tend to interpret the beast as a reflection of the medieval mythological view on giraffes, whose generic name of Camelopardalis originated from their description of being half-camel and half-leopard.",

      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {

    },
    {
      "id": "questing-beast-c04",
      "source_id": "questing-beast-s1",
      "quote": "Merlin reveals that the monster had been born of a human woman, a princess who lusted after her own brother.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Post-Vulgate, Merlin: makhluk itu lahir dari putri yang bernafsu kepada saudaranya.",

    },
    {
      "id": "questing-beast-c05",
      "source_id": "questing-beast-s1",
      "quote": "Before he died, he prophesied that his sister would give birth to an abomination that would make the same sounds as the pack of dogs that were about to kill him.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Saudara itu menubuatkan makhluk keji bersuara kawanan anjing.",
        "en": "The brother foretold an abomination sounding like the dogs."

      "id": "questing-beast-c06",
      "source_id": "questing-beast-s2",
      "quote": "Das Glatisant, auch Questentier oder bellendes Tier genannt, ist eine bizarr anmutende Kreatur der mythischen Welt von König Artus",

    },

      "id": "questing-beast-c07",
      "source_id": "questing-beast-s2",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Water horse (kuda air) adalah makhluk mitos yang muncul dalam berbagai bentuk dalam cerita rakyat Kelt dan Nordik, seperti kelpie, each-uisge, dan bäckahästen.",
    "en": "The water horse is a mythical creature found in many forms in Celtic and Nordic folklore, such as the kelpie, each-uisge and bäckahästen.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "water-horse-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Water horse (juga waterhorse) adalah makhluk mitos yang muncul dalam berbagai bentuk dalam cerita rakyat Kelt (Celtic) dan Nordik (Nordic). Wujud dalam tradisi Kelt antara lain Ceffyl Dŵr dari Wales (Welsh), each uisce dari Irlandia (Irish), serta each-uisge dan kelpie dari Skotlandia (Scottish). Dalam cerita rakyat Nordik, kuda air adalah wujud yang diambil nixie, yang dalam bahasa Swedia (Swedish) disebut bäckahästen (\"kuda sungai kecil\") dan dalam bahasa Faroe (Faroese) nykur.",
      "en": "The water horse (also waterhorse) is a mythical being appearing in many guises in Celtic and Nordic folklore. Celtic forms include the Welsh Ceffyl Dŵr, the Irish each uisce, and the Scottish each-uisge and kelpie. In Nordic lore the water horse is a shape the nixie takes, called bäckahästen ('the brook horse') in Swedish and nykur in Faroese.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "water-horse-c01"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Istilah \"water horse\" mulanya nama bagi kelpie, dan juga dipakai sebagai julukan monster danau seperti Ogopogo dan Nessie. Penggunaan \"water horse\" dan \"kelpie\" sering membingungkan: sebagian menganggapnya sinonim, sementara yang lain membedakan kuda air sebagai penghuni danau (loch) dan kelpie sebagai penghuni air deras seperti sungai, arungan, dan air terjun. Menurut artikel de, kuda air dikenal dari berbagai budaya dan zaman, termasuk Denmark, Yunani, Irlandia, dan Portugal, dan gagasan roh sungai berwujud kuda mungkin berasal dari kurban kuda di perairan.",
      "en": "'Water horse' was first a name for the kelpie and has also served as a nickname for lake monsters such as Ogopogo and Nessie. The terms 'water horse' and 'kelpie' are easily confused: some treat them as the same, while others make the water horse a dweller of lochs and the kelpie a creature of rough water such as rivers, fords and waterfalls. According to the de article water horses are known from many cultures and eras, including Denmark, Greece, Ireland and Portugal, and the idea of horse-shaped river spirits may go back to sacrificing horses in water.",

        "en": "The water horse (waterhorse) is a mythical creature of Celtic and Nordic folklore: Welsh Ceffyl Dŵr, Irish each uisce, Scottish each-uisge and kelpie; a nixie's shape, Swedish bäckahästen, Faroese nykur."

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Wild haggis adalah makhluk rekaan jenaka dalam cerita rakyat Skotlandia, konon asli Dataran Tinggi Skotlandia dan menjadi sumber hidangan haggis.",
    "en": "The wild haggis is a humorous fictional creature of Scottish folklore, said to be native to the Highlands and the source of the dish haggis.",

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Wild haggis (dengan nama taksonomi jenaka Haggis scoticus) adalah makhluk rekaan cerita rakyat Skotlandia (Scottish folklore) yang konon asli Dataran Tinggi Skotlandia (Scottish Highlands). Secara bercanda ia diklaim sebagai sumber haggis, hidangan tradisional Skotlandia yang sebenarnya dibuat dari jeroan domba seperti jantung, paru-paru, dan hati. Menurut sebagian sumber, kaki kiri dan kanannya berbeda panjang, seperti sidehill gouger dan dahu, sehingga ia dapat berlari cepat mengelilingi gunung dan lereng terjal habitatnya, tetapi hanya ke satu arah.",
      "en": "The wild haggis (jokingly classified as Haggis scoticus) is a made-up creature of Scottish folklore, supposedly native to the Scottish Highlands. It is claimed in jest to be where haggis comes from, though that traditional Scottish dish is really made from sheep's innards such as heart, lungs and liver. Some say its left and right legs differ in length, like those of the sidehill gouger and the dahu, so it can race round the steep mountains and slopes it lives on, but only one way.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wild-haggis-c01",
        "wild-haggis-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Konon ada dua varietas haggis, satu berkaki kiri lebih panjang dan satu berkaki kanan lebih panjang, yang berlari searah atau berlawanan arah jarum jam mengelilingi gunung. Keduanya hidup damai tetapi tak dapat kawin silang di alam liar, karena jantan harus berbalik menghadap arah yang sama dengan betina dan kehilangan keseimbangan. Menurut survei daring yang dipesan produsen haggis Hall's of Broxburn pada 2003, sepertiga pengunjung AS ke Skotlandia percaya wild haggis makhluk nyata. Menurut artikel de, ia digambarkan sebagai hewan kecil berbulu mirip persilangan kelinci dan tupai.",
      "en": "There are said to be two varieties, one with longer left legs and one with longer right legs, running clockwise or anticlockwise round a mountain. They live in peace but cannot interbreed in the wild, since a male would have to turn to face the female's way and lose his balance. In an online survey commissioned by haggis maker Hall's of Broxburn in 2003, a third of US visitors to Scotland believed the wild haggis was real. According to the de article it is pictured as a small furry animal like a cross between a rabbit and a squirrel.",

      "quote": "According to an online survey commissioned by haggis manufacturers Hall's of Broxburn, released on 26 November 2003, one-third of U.S. visitors to Scotland believed the wild haggis to be a real creature.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Survei Hall's of Broxburn 2003: sepertiga pengunjung AS (U.S.) ke Skotlandia (Scotland) percaya ia nyata.",
        "en": "A 2003 Hall's of Broxburn survey: a third of U.S. visitors to Scotland believed it real."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wild-haggis-c05",
      "source_id": "wild-haggis-s2",
      "quote": "Das Wilde Haggis (humoristischer wissenschaftlicher Name: Haggis scoticus) ist ein fiktives kleines Säugetier der schottischen Folklore, das angeblich in den Highlands beheimatet ist.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wildes Haggis (Haggis scoticus) mamalia kecil rekaan (fiktives kleines Säugetier) folklore Skotlandia (schottischen), di Highlands.",
        "en": "The Wildes Haggis (Haggis scoticus) is a fictional small mammal (fiktives kleines Säugetier) of Scottish (schottischen) folklore, said to live in the Highlands."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wild-haggis-c06",
      "source_id": "wild-haggis-s2",
      "quote": "Das Wilde Haggis, eine in Schottland endemische Art, ist ein kleines, pelziges Tier, das einer Kreuzung aus Kaninchen und Eichhörnchen ähneln soll.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hewan kecil berbulu mirip persilangan kelinci (Kaninchen) dan tupai (Eichhörnchen), endemik Skotlandia (Schottland).",
        "en": "A small furry animal like a cross of rabbit (Kaninchen) and squirrel (Eichhörnchen), endemic to Scotland (Schottland)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wild-haggis-c07",
      "source_id": "wild-haggis-s1",
      "quote": "It is further claimed that there are two varieties of haggis, one with longer left legs and the other with longer right legs. The former variety can run clockwise around a mountain (as seen from above) while the latter can run anticlockwise.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dua varietas: kaki kiri atau kaki kanan lebih panjang, lari searah atau berlawanan jarum jam.",
        "en": "Two varieties: longer left or right legs, running clockwise or anticlockwise."
      }
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Ysbaddaden Bencawr (\"Ysbaddaden, Kepala Para Raksasa\") adalah raksasa kejam dan tokoh antagonis utama roman Wales Culhwch and Olwen, ayah Olwen.",
    "en": "Ysbaddaden Bencawr ('Ysbaddaden, Chief of Giants') is the vicious giant and main antagonist of the Welsh romance Culhwch and Olwen, father of Olwen.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "ysbaddaden-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Ysbaddaden Bencawr, \"Ysbaddaden, Kepala Para Raksasa\" (dari akar kata yang berarti \"hawthorn\" atau \"mandul\", atau mungkin keduanya), adalah antagonis utama roman Wales (Welsh) Culhwch and Olwen. Ia raksasa kejam yang tinggal di kastel yang hampir tak terjangkau, ayah Olwen dan paman Goreu fab Custennin. Ibu tiri Culhwch mengutuknya agar hanya dapat menikahi Olwen, putri Ysbaddaden, sehingga Culhwch mencari bantuan sepupunya Arthur. Ysbaddaden telah merampas tanah saudaranya, gembala Custennin, dan membunuh dua puluh tiga anaknya karena kekejaman.",
      "en": "Known as Ysbaddaden Bencawr, 'Chief of Giants', a name whose roots may mean 'hawthorn', 'infertile' or both, he is the chief villain of the Welsh romance Culhwch and Olwen. A cruel giant in a castle almost impossible to reach, he is Olwen's father and uncle of Goreu fab Custennin. Culhwch's stepmother curses him to wed no one but Olwen, Ysbaddaden's daughter, so Culhwch seeks help from his cousin Arthur. Ysbaddaden had seized the lands of his brother, the shepherd Custennin, and out of cruelty killed twenty-three of his children.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ysbaddaden-c01",
        "ysbaddaden-c02",
        "ysbaddaden-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ketika Culhwch dan kawan-kawannya memasuki aulanya, Ysbaddaden mencoba membunuh Culhwch dengan anak panah beracun tetapi dikalahkan dan dilukai oleh Bedwyr, penyihir Menw, dan Culhwch sendiri. Akhirnya ia setuju menyerahkan putrinya dengan syarat Culhwch menyelesaikan sejumlah tugas mustahil (anoethau), termasuk memburu Twrch Trwyth. Menurut artikel de, kelopak matanya begitu berat sehingga seorang pelayan harus mengangkatnya dengan garpu bila ia ingin melihat seseorang (bandingkan Balor), dan kastelnya bergerbang sembilan.",
      "en": "When Culhwch and his companions enter his hall, Ysbaddaden tries to kill Culhwch with a poisoned dart but is outwitted and wounded by Bedwyr, the enchanter Menw and Culhwch himself. At last he agrees to give up his daughter if Culhwch completes a series of impossible tasks (anoethau), among them hunting Twrch Trwyth. According to the de article his eyelids are so heavy that a servant must lift them with a fork when he wants to look at someone (compare Balor), and his castle has nine gates.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Beast of Bodmin adalah hewan mirip kucing besar yang konon terlihat di Bodmin Moor, Cornwall, Inggris, dan dianggap sebagai salah satu \"British big cats\".",
    "en": "The Beast of Bodmin is a big-cat-like animal reportedly seen on Bodmin Moor in Cornwall, England, counted among the 'British big cats'.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "beast-of-bodmin-c01",
      "beast-of-bodmin-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Beast of Bodmin (atau Beast of Bodmin Moor) adalah salah satu kucing besar Britania (British big cats) yang paling terkenal, yang dituduh membunuh ternak. Menurut artikel ru, hewan mirip kucing besar ini terlihat lebih dari 60 kali sejak 1983 di Bodmin Moor, Cornwall, Inggris, dan diduga panther atau puma yang lepas dari kebun binatang atau koleksi pribadi. Penyelidikan pemerintah pada 1995 tidak menemukan bukti keberadaan hewan semacam itu, meski laporan penampakan terus berlanjut. Menurut artikel it, makhluk itu konon mirip puma berwarna abu-abu gelap hingga hitam dan juga terlihat di Devon dan Somerset.",
      "en": "The Beast of Bodmin (or Beast of Bodmin Moor) is one of the best-known British big cats, blamed for killing livestock. According to the ru article this big-cat-like animal has been seen more than 60 times since 1983 on Bodmin Moor in Cornwall, England, and is thought to be a panther or puma escaped from a zoo or private collection. A government inquiry in 1995 found no evidence of such animals, though sightings continued. According to the it article it is said to look like a puma, dark grey to black, and has also been reported in Devon and Somerset.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "beast-of-bodmin-c01",
        "beast-of-bodmin-c02",
        "beast-of-bodmin-c03",
        "beast-of-bodmin-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ketika sebuah tengkorak yang ditemukan di Sungai Fowey diserahkan kepada Natural History Museum sebagai bukti keberadaannya, tengkorak itu ternyata dipotong dari permadani kulit macan tutul. Menurut artikel ru, tengkorak yang ditemukan pada 1995 itu memang milik macan tutul jantan muda, tetapi hewan itu tidak dibunuh di Inggris. Para ilmuwan menyatakan laporan semacam itu biasanya tidak dapat dipercaya, karena kelangsungan populasi memerlukan jumlah individu yang tak masuk akal, sedangkan iklim dan sulitnya mencari makan membuat hewan yang lepas sulit bertahan.",
      "en": "When a skull found in the River Fowey was offered to the Natural History Museum as proof of the beast, it turned out to have been cut from a leopard-skin rug. According to the ru article that skull, found in 1995, did belong to a young male leopard, but the animal had not been killed in England. Scientists say such reports are usually unreliable, since a lasting population would need an implausible number of animals, while the climate and scarce prey make survival of escaped cats unlikely.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "beast-of-bodmin-c04",
        "beast-of-bodmin-c06",
        "beast-of-bodmin-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bodmin-c01",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bodmin-s1",
      "quote": "unexplained animal deaths, typically livestock, would be blamed on such alleged creatures, like the Beast of Bodmin Moor and the Cotswolds Big Cat.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kematian hewan, terutama ternak, dituduhkan pada makhluk seperti Beast of Bodmin Moor; British big cats (kucing besar Britania).",
        "en": "Unexplained livestock deaths were blamed on creatures like the Beast of Bodmin Moor; British big cats."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bodmin-c02",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bodmin-s2",
      "quote": "Бодминский зверь — похожее на большую кошку животное, которое с 1983 года видели более 60 раз в местности Бодмин-Мур (Корнуолл, Англия).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel ru, Бодминский зверь hewan mirip kucing besar, terlihat lebih dari 60 kali sejak 1983 di Bodmin Moor (Бодмин-Мур), Cornwall, Inggris (Англия); kriptid; di Britania Raya.",
        "en": "Per the ru article the Бодминский зверь is a big-cat-like animal seen over 60 times since 1983 on Bodmin Moor (Бодмин-Мур), Cornwall, England (Англия)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bodmin-c03",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bodmin-s2",
      "quote": "Расследование, проведённое правительством в 1995 году, не выявило никаких свидетельств присутствия подобных животных, однако встречи с животным продолжались",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penyelidikan pemerintah 1995 tak menemukan bukti; penampakan berlanjut.",
        "en": "A 1995 government inquiry found no evidence; sightings continued."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bodmin-c04",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bodmin-s1",
      "quote": "In the case of the Beast of Bodmin, when a skull found in the River Fowey was presented to the Natural History Museum as proof of its existence, it was determined to have been cut from a leopard skin rug",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tengkorak dari River Fowey yang diserahkan ke Natural History Museum ternyata dari permadani kulit macan tutul.",
        "en": "A skull from the River Fowey given to the Natural History Museum came from a leopard skin rug."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bodmin-c05",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bodmin-s3",
      "quote": "La Bestia di Bodmin, nota anche come Bestia della Brughiera di Bodmin, è un presunto grosso felino; si dice che sia stato avvistato, appunto, nella Brughiera di Bodmin in Cornovaglia, ma anche in Devon e Somerset. Simile a un puma, dovrebbe essere di colore grigio scuro-nero",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bestia di Bodmin, felin besar, terlihat di Cornwall (Cornovaglia), Devon, Somerset; mirip puma, abu-abu gelap hingga hitam.",
        "en": "The Bestia di Bodmin, a supposed big cat seen in Cornwall (Cornovaglia), Devon and Somerset; puma-like, dark grey to black."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bodmin-c06",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bodmin-s2",
      "quote": "Сотрудники Музея естествознания, исследовавшие череп, определили, что он действительно принадлежал молодому самцу леопарда, однако было установлено, что животное не было убито в Англии",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tengkorak (1995) milik macan tutul jantan muda, tapi tak dibunuh di Inggris (Англии).",
        "en": "The skull (1995) belonged to a young male leopard, but it was not killed in England (Англии)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bodmin-c07",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bodmin-s2",
      "quote": "Учёные указывают, что обычно сообщения о обнаружении подобных животных являются недостоверными, поскольку для поддержания их численности необходимо наличие неправдоподобно большого числа особей в популяции.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ilmuwan: laporan semacam itu biasanya tak dapat dipercaya; populasi perlu jumlah tak masuk akal; iklim dan makanan menyulitkan.",
        "en": "Scientists: such reports are usually unreliable; a population would need implausible numbers."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Damona adalah dewi penyembuhan yang dipuja di Galia Romawi, terutama di tempat suci mata air panas Burgundy, dan paling sering berpasangan dengan dewa mata air Borvo.",
    "en": "Damona was a healing goddess worshipped in Roman Gaul, chiefly at the hot-spring sanctuaries of Burgundy, most often as consort of the spring god Borvo.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "damona-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Damona adalah dewi yang dipuja di Galia Romawi (Roman Gaul) dan hanya dikenal dari prasasti persembahan Latin masa Romawi. Persembahan untuknya berasal dari tempat suci mata air panas di Galia timur-tengah, terutama Burgundy kini, tempat ia dihormati sebagai dewi penyembuh air berkhasiat. Ia paling sering muncul sebagai pasangan dewa mata air Borvo, tetapi juga dipasangkan dengan dewa Albius dan Apollo Moritasgus, dan juga dipuja sendirian. Namanya umumnya diturunkan dari kata Kelt (Celtic) *damo- yang terkait dengan hewan seperti lembu dan rusa, dengan akhiran -ona seperti Epona, Matrona, dan Sirona.",
      "en": "Damona was a goddess of Roman Gaul known only from Latin votive inscriptions of the Roman era. They come from hot-spring sanctuaries of east-central Gaul, mostly in today's Burgundy, where she was revered as a healer of the curative waters. Usually she stands beside Borvo, god of springs, yet inscriptions also couple her with Albius or Apollo Moritasgus, and some name her alone. Her name is usually traced to a Celtic *damo- linked with cattle and deer in later languages, ending in the -ona seen in Epona, Matrona and Sirona.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "damona-c01",
        "damona-c02",
        "damona-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia termasuk kelompok dewi Galia untuk mata air penyembuh; di setiap tempat namanya disebut, latarnya adalah tempat suci penyembuhan di sumber air panas atau mineral. Miranda Aldhouse-Green menganggap Alesia sebagai tempat suci utamanya, tempat ia dipuja bersama Apollo Moritasgus di kawasan dengan mata air suci dan pemandian bagi orang sakit; di sana ditemukan pecahan arcanya: kepala batu bermahkota bulir gandum. Menurut artikel de, ia pasangan dewa penyembuh Bormo dan karena itu juga disebut Bormana, dan Birkhan menduga ia nimfa mata air yang dipuja dalam wujud rusa betina.",
      "en": "She is one of the Gaulish goddesses of healing springs; every site naming her is a curative sanctuary at hot or mineral waters. Miranda Aldhouse-Green sees Alesia as her main sanctuary, where she was worshipped with Apollo Moritasgus in a precinct of sacred springs and baths for the sick; a fragment of her cult statue found there shows a stone head crowned with ears of grain. According to the de article she was the partner of the healing god Bormo and so also called Bormana, and Birkhan thought her a spring nymph worshipped in the form of a hind.",

    "id": "Grindylow adalah makhluk air dalam cerita rakyat Inggris di Yorkshire dan Lancashire, humanoid kecil bersisik yang menyeret anak-anak ke dasar kolam dan rawa.",
    "en": "The grindylow is a water creature of English folklore in Yorkshire and Lancashire, a small scaly humanoid that drags children under ponds and marshes.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "grindylow-c01",
      "grindylow-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Inggris (English folklore), Grindylow atau Grundylow adalah makhluk dari county Yorkshire dan Lancashire. Namanya diduga berhubungan dengan Grendel, nama atau istilah yang dipakai dalam Beowulf dan banyak piagam bahasa Inggris Kuno (Old English) dalam kaitannya dengan danau, rawa, dan telaga. Grindylow adalah makhluk gaib cerita rakyat Inggris, terutama di daerah Lancaster, digambarkan sebagai humanoid kecil bersisik dan kehijauan, bercakar dan bergigi tajam, dengan lengan panjang kurus berjari panjang.",
      "en": "In English folklore the Grindylow or Grundylow is a creature of the counties of Yorkshire and Lancashire. Its name is thought to be linked to Grendel, a name or term found in Beowulf and in many Old English charters in connection with meres, bogs and lakes. Grindylows are supernatural beings of English folklore, especially around Lancaster, pictured as tiny humanoids with scaly greenish skin, sharp claws and teeth, and long wiry arms ending in long fingers.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "grindylow-c01",
        "grindylow-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Mereka dikatakan tinggal di kolam dan rawa menunggu anak-anak yang lengah, menangkapnya dengan cengkeraman yang luar biasa kuat, lalu menyeretnya ke bawah permukaan air. Grindylow dipakai sebagai sosok bayangan untuk menakuti anak-anak agar menjauhi kolam dan rawa tempat mereka bisa tenggelam; Peg Powler, Nelly Longarms, dan Jenny Greenteeth adalah roh air serupa. Menurut artikel fr, grindylow menangkap anak-anak dengan lengannya yang panjang dan membawa mereka ke dasar danau dan kolam tempat ia tinggal.",
      "en": "They are said to lurk in ponds and marshes for careless children, seizing them with an astonishingly strong grip and pulling them beneath the water. Grindylows served as shadowy figures to scare children away from pools, marshes and ponds where they might drown; comparable water spirits include Peg Powler, Nelly Longarms and Jenny Greenteeth. According to the fr article the grindylow grabs children with its long arms and carries them to the bottom of the lakes and ponds where it lives.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "grindylow-c03",
        "grindylow-c04",
        "grindylow-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Lludd Llaw Eraint (\"Lludd of the Silver Hand\"), putra Beli Mawr, adalah pahlawan legenda mitologi Wales, raja Britania dalam kisah Lludd and Llefelys.",
    "en": "Son of Beli Mawr and called Lludd of the Silver Hand, this Welsh legendary hero is king of Britain in the tale of Lludd and Llefelys.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "lludd-llaw-eraint-c01",

  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Lludd Llaw Ereint (\"Lludd of the Silver Hand\", Lludd bertangan perak), putra Beli Mawr, adalah pahlawan legenda dari mitologi Wales (Welsh). Sebagai Nudd Llaw Ereint, bentuk lebih tua namanya yang sekerabat dengan Nuada Airgetlám dari Irlandia (Irish) dan berasal dari dewa Kelt (Celtic) pra-Romawi Nodens, ia ayah Gwyn ap Nudd. Ia kemungkinan sumber raja Lud dalam History of the Kings of Britain karya Geoffrey of Monmouth. Dalam kisah Mabinogion Lludd and Llefelys, ia penguasa Britania (Britain) sementara saudaranya Llefelys memerintah Galia (Gaul), dan Lludd meminta Llefelys membebaskan Britania dari tiga wabah.",
      "en": "A legendary hero of Welsh myth, Lludd Llaw Ereint, 'of the Silver Hand', was a son of Beli Mawr. As Nudd Llaw Ereint, the older form of his name, akin to the Irish Nuada Airgetlám and rooted in the pre-Roman Celtic god Nodens, he is Gwyn ap Nudd's father. King Lud, as told by Geoffrey of Monmouth in his History of the Kings of Britain, probably derives from him. In the Mabinogion tale of Lludd and Llefelys he rules Britain while his brother Llefelys rules Gaul, and Lludd asks Llefelys to rid Britain of three plagues.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lludd-llaw-eraint-c01",
        "lludd-llaw-eraint-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Wabah pertama adalah Coranians, bangsa kerdil \"dari Asia\" yang mungkin mewakili bangsa Romawi. Wabah kedua adalah naga putih dan naga merah, yang mewakili bangsa Briton dan penyerbu Anglo-Saxon; pada malam May Day keduanya bertarung dan jeritan naga merah membuat negeri mandul. Wabah itu diatasi dengan menangkap kedua naga di Oxford, pusat pulau itu, dan menguburkan mereka di lubang batu di Dinas Emrys, Snowdonia. Menurut artikel de, Lludd dianggap pendiri legendaris London (Caer Lludd, Llundain), dan putrinya Creiddylad menjadi teladan bagi Cordelia karya Shakespeare.",
      "en": "The first plague was the Coranians, a race of dwarfs 'from Asia' who may stand for the Romans. The second was the white and red dragons, standing for the Brythons and the Anglo-Saxon invaders; on May Day eve they fought and the red dragon's scream left the land barren. The plague ended when both dragons were caught at Oxford, the island's centre, and buried in a rock pit at Dinas Emrys in Snowdonia. According to the de article Lludd is held to be the legendary founder of London (Caer Lludd, Llundain), and his daughter Creiddylad was the model for Shakespeare's Cordelia.",

      "id": "lludd-llaw-eraint-c07",
      "source_id": "lludd-llaw-eraint-s2",
      "quote": "Lludd Llawereint hat in der Erzählung Mal y kavas Kulhwch Olwen („Wie Kulhwch Olwen errungen hat“) Creiddylad zur Tochter, das Vorbild für Shakespeares Cordelia",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Putrinya Creiddylad teladan Cordelia karya Shakespeare (Shakespeares).",
        "en": "His daughter Creiddylad was the model for Shakespeare's (Shakespeares) Cordelia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lludd-llaw-eraint-c08",
      "source_id": "lludd-llaw-eraint-s1",
      "quote": "Philological connection suggests that there was once a memorial to Lludd at the site of St Paul's Cathedral, London, near Ludgate, which is named after him.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ludgate di London dinamai menurut Lludd; dekat St Paul's Cathedral.",
        "en": "Ludgate in London is named after Lludd; near St Paul's Cathedral."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lludd-llaw-eraint-c09",
      "source_id": "lludd-llaw-eraint-s1",
      "quote": "This place was at Oxford, which Lludd found to be the exact centre of the island when he measured the island of Britain.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Naga ditangkap di Oxford, pusat pulau Britania (Britain).",
        "en": "The dragons were caught at Oxford, the exact centre of Britain."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Merrow adalah putri duyung atau duyung lelaki dalam cerita rakyat Irlandia, yang konon memerlukan topi ajaib untuk berpindah antara laut dalam dan daratan.",
    "en": "The merrow is a mermaid or merman of Irish folklore, said to need a magic cap to pass between deep water and dry land.",
    "claim_ids": [

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Merrow (dari Irlandia/Irish murúch; Irlandia Pertengahan/Middle Irish murdúchann atau murdúchu) adalah putri duyung atau duyung lelaki dalam cerita rakyat Irlandia (Irish folklore). Istilah ini adalah bentuk Inggris dari kata Irlandia murúch. Merrow konon memerlukan topi ajaib (cochaillín draíochta; dianglisisasi cohuleen druith) untuk berpindah antara laut dalam dan daratan. Sarjana masa kini menganggap merrow istilah Hiberno-Inggris (Hiberno-English) dari murúch yang berarti \"penyanyi laut\" atau \"siren\".",
      "en": "In Irish folklore a merrow, from Irish murúch (Middle Irish murdúchann or murdúchu), is a sea-maid or sea-man. The English word comes from the Irish murúch. Merrows supposedly need a magic cap (cochaillín draíochta, anglicised cohuleen druith) to move between the deep sea and dry land. Present-day scholars treat merrow as a Hiberno-English word from murúch, meaning 'sea singer' or 'siren'.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "merrow-c01",
        "merrow-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Istilah ini muncul dalam dua kisah berlatar Irlandia (Ireland) abad ke-19: \"Lady of Gollerus\", tentang merrow berambut hijau yang menikah dengan lelaki Kerry yang menyembunyikan topi merah ajaibnya, dan \"The Soul Cages\", tentang merrow lelaki bertubuh hijau yang menjamu nelayan di rumahnya di bawah laut. Kisah-kisah itu pertama kali terbit dalam Fairy Legends (1828) karya T. C. Croker, dan W. B. Yeats serta yang lain banyak meminjam darinya; \"The Soul Cages\" ternyata rekaan Thomas Keightley. Menurut artikel de, merrow manusia dari kepala hingga pinggang dan berbadan ikan dari pinggang ke bawah, berwatak ramah, sederhana, penyayang, dan baik hati.",
      "en": "The term appears in two 19th-century tales set in Ireland: 'Lady of Gollerus', where a green-haired merrow marries a Kerry man who hides her magic red cap, and 'The Soul Cages', where a green-bodied male merrow entertains a fisherman in his home under the sea. These tales first appeared in T. C. Croker's Fairy Legends (1828), and W. B. Yeats and others borrowed heavily from it; 'The Soul Cages' proved to be fiction by Thomas Keightley. According to the de article merrows are human from head to waist with a fish's body below, and are friendly, modest, loving and kind.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "merrow-c02",

  "disposition": null,
  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Ritona (juga Pritona, bahasa Galia untuk \"dia dari arungan\") adalah dewi Kelt kuno di Galia Romawi, terutama di antara suku Treveri, yang umumnya ditafsirkan sebagai dewi arungan dan penyeberangan sungai.",
    "en": "Called Ritona or Pritona, a Gaulish name meaning 'she of the ford', this old Celtic goddess was honoured in Roman Gaul, mainly by the Treveri, as a guardian of fords and river crossings.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "ritona-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Ritona (juga Pritona; bahasa Galia/Gaulish untuk \"dia dari arungan\") adalah dewi Kelt (Celtic) kuno yang dipuja di Galia Romawi (Roman Gaul), terutama di antara suku Treveri di Moselle, dan umumnya ditafsirkan sebagai dewi arungan dan penyeberangan sungai. Pusat pemujaan utamanya di Augusta Treverorum (Trier kini), tempat ia memiliki kuil di tempat suci lembah Altbach; ia juga tercatat di desa Contiomagus (Pachten, Saar kini) dan di Galia selatan. Namanya dibangun dari kata Galia untuk arungan, ritu-, yang berlanjut dalam bahasa Wales rhyd, Cornwall rid, dan Breton rit.",
      "en": "Ritona, also written Pritona, bears a Gaulish name meaning 'she of the ford'; this old Celtic goddess of Roman Gaul was honoured above all by the Treveri of the Moselle and generally seen as a goddess of fords and river crossings. Her chief cult centre was Augusta Treverorum, today's Trier, whose Altbach valley sanctuary held a temple of hers; she is also recorded at the village of Contiomagus (now Pachten, Saar) and in southern Gaul. Her name rests on the Gaulish word for ford, ritu-, which lives on in Welsh rhyd, Cornish rid and Breton rit.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ritona-c01",
        "ritona-c02",
        "ritona-c03",
        "ritona-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia termasuk kelompok dewa air dan mata air Treveri yang terutama dikenal dari prasasti, seperti Icovellauna. Paul-Marie Duval dan Lothar Schwinden menganggap Pritona bentuk lebih tua yang mempertahankan huruf p awal. Menurut artikel de, sebuah prasasti persembahan untuknya juga ditemukan di Uzès di Prancis selatan, dan Ritona adalah dewi pelindung aliran air dan arungan Sungai Saar di Contiomagus.",
      "en": "She is one of a group of Treveran water and spring deities known mainly from inscriptions, Icovellauna among them. Paul-Marie Duval and Lothar Schwinden take Pritona to be the older form, keeping the initial p. According to the de article a dedication to her was also found at Uzès in southern France, and Ritona was the protective goddess of the watercourse and the ford across the Saar at Contiomagus.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ritona-c04",
        "ritona-c07",
        "ritona-c05",
        "ritona-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ritona (Pritona; Galia, Gaulish: dia dari arungan) dewi Kelt (Celtic) Galia Romawi (Roman Gaul), suku Treveri, Moselle; dewi arungan; kuil di Augusta Treverorum (Trier), Altbach; di Jerman dan Prancis.",

      "quote": "Her name is built on the Gaulish word for 'ford' and occurs in two variant forms, Ritona and Pritona.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama dari kata Galia (Gaulish) untuk arungan; dua bentuk Ritona dan Pritona.",
        "en": "The name is from the Gaulish word for ford; two forms Ritona and Pritona."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ritona-c03",
      "source_id": "ritona-s1",
      "quote": "The inherited initial p was lost, as regularly in Celtic, and the reflex ritu- survives in Welsh rhyd, Cornish rid and Breton rit ('ford').",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "ritu- bertahan dalam Wales (Welsh) rhyd, Cornwall (Cornish) rid, Breton rit (arungan).",
        "en": "ritu- survives in Welsh rhyd, Cornish rid and Breton rit (ford)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ritona-c04",
      "source_id": "ritona-s1",
      "quote": "Ritona belongs to a group of Treveran deities of water and springs known chiefly from inscriptions, among them Icovellauna, who was honoured at Trier and at Metz.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kelompok dewa air dan mata air Treveri (Treveran) seperti Icovellauna.",
        "en": "A group of Treveran water and spring deities such as Icovellauna."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ritona-c05",
      "source_id": "ritona-s2",
      "quote": "Ritona, auch Pritona war eine keltische Göttin, der im vicus Contiomagus (Pachten, Ortsteil von Dillingen/Saar) und im Tempelbezirk im Altbachtal in Augusta Treverorum (Trier) ein Tempel gewidmet war. Aber auch im südfranzösischen Uzès wurde eine Weiheinschrift für sie gefunden.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ritona (Pritona) dewi Kelt (keltische Göttin), kuil di Contiomagus (Pachten) dan Altbachtal, Trier; prasasti di Uzès, Prancis selatan (südfranzösischen).",
        "en": "Ritona (Pritona) was a Celtic goddess (keltische Göttin) with temples at Contiomagus (Pachten) and the Altbachtal, Trier; a dedication at Uzès in southern France (südfranzösischen)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ritona-c06",
      "source_id": "ritona-s2",
      "quote": "Ritona war eine Schutzgöttin des Wasserlaufes und der Furt (-ritum „Furt“) durch die Saar bei Contiomagus.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dewi pelindung (Schutzgöttin) aliran air dan arungan (Furt) Sungai Saar di Contiomagus.",
        "en": "Protective goddess (Schutzgöttin) of the watercourse and ford (Furt) across the Saar at Contiomagus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ritona-c07",
      "source_id": "ritona-s1",
      "quote": "Paul-Marie Duval and Lothar Schwinden take Pritona to be the earlier form, keeping the initial p- of the archaic name.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Paul-Marie Duval dan Lothar Schwinden: Pritona bentuk lebih tua.",
        "en": "Paul-Marie Duval and Lothar Schwinden: Pritona is the earlier form."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ritona-c08",
      "source_id": "ritona-s1",
      "quote": "She is also attested at the village of Contiomagus (modern Pachten, Saar) and in southern Gaul.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Juga tercatat di Contiomagus (Pachten, Saar) dan Galia selatan.",
        "en": "Also attested at Contiomagus (Pachten, Saar) and southern Gaul."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Tarvos Trigaranus (\"banteng dengan tiga bangau\") adalah sosok ilahi Galia yang muncul pada relief Pillar of the Boatmen sebagai banteng dengan tiga burung bangau di punggungnya.",
    "en": "Tarvos Trigaranus ('bull with three cranes') is a Gaulish sacred figure carved on the Pillar of the Boatmen: a bull carrying three cranes.",

    {
      "id": "Tarvos Trigaranus atau Taruos Trigaranos adalah sosok ilahi yang muncul pada panel relief Pillar of the Boatmen sebagai banteng dengan tiga burung bangau bertengger di punggungnya. Ia berdiri di bawah pohon, dan pada panel di sebelahnya dewa Esus sedang menebang pohon, mungkin pohon dedalu, dengan kapak. Dalam bahasa Galia (Gaulish), taruos berarti \"banteng\", seperti Irlandia Kuno (Old Irish) tarb dan Wales (Welsh) tarw; garanus berarti burung bangau, dan tri- berarti tiga.",
      "en": "Tarvos Trigaranus or Taruos Trigaranos is a divine figure carved on a panel of the Pillar of the Boatmen: a bull bearing three cranes, standing beneath a tree. On the neighbouring panel the god Esus cuts down a tree, maybe a willow, with an axe. In Gaulish taruos means 'bull', as Old Irish tarb and Welsh tarw; garanus is the crane, and tri- means three.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "tarvos-trigaranus-c01",
        "tarvos-trigaranus-c02",

    {
      "id": "Sebuah pilar dari Trier menampilkan lelaki berkapak menebang pohon tempat tiga burung dan kepala banteng berada. Di kuil Galia-Romawi Maiden Castle ditemukan patung perunggu kecil banteng.",
      "en": "On a pillar from Trier an axe-wielding man fells a tree holding a bull's head and three birds.   "short_description": {
    "id": "Black Shuck adalah anjing hitam hantu dalam cerita rakyat Inggris yang konon berkeliaran di pesisir dan pedesaan East Anglia, kadang dianggap pertanda kematian.",
    "en": "Black Shuck is a ghostly black dog of English folklore said to roam the coast and countryside of East Anglia, sometimes taken as an omen of death.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "black-shuck-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Inggris (English folklore), Black Shuck, Old Shuck, Old Shock, atau Shuck adalah nama anjing hitam hantu yang konon berkeliaran di pesisir dan pedesaan East Anglia, salah satu dari banyak anjing hitam dalam cerita rakyat di Kepulauan Britania (British Isles). Kisahnya bagian dari cerita rakyat Norfolk, Suffolk, Cambridgeshire Fens, dan Essex, dan gambaran rupanya sangat beragam; kadang ia pertanda kematian, kadang justru teman yang ramah. Menurut Oxford English Dictionary, nama Shuck berasal dari kata Inggris Kuno (Old English) scucca, \"setan\".",
      "en": "In English folklore Black Shuck, Old Shuck, Old Shock or just Shuck is a ghostly black dog said to wander the coast and countryside of East Anglia, one of many black dogs in the folklore of the British Isles. His tales belong to Norfolk, Suffolk, the Cambridgeshire Fens and Essex, and accounts of his looks and nature differ widely; sometimes he foretells death, while elsewhere he is a friendly companion. The Oxford English Dictionary derives Shuck from Old English scucca, 'devil, fiend'.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "black-shuck-c01",
        "black-shuck-c02",
        "black-shuck-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Penyebutan tertulis pertama \"Black Shuck\" oleh Pendeta E. S. Taylor pada 1850 dalam Notes and Queries menggambarkannya sebagai anjing hitam berbulu lebat, bermata api, berukuran sangat besar, yang mengunjungi pekuburan pada tengah malam. Catatan Abraham Fleming tentang kemunculannya di Bungay, Suffolk, pada 1577 adalah kisah terkenal tentang makhluk itu. W. A. Dutt (1901) menulis bahwa ia hanya bermata satu di tengah kepala seperti Cyclops, dan bertemu dengannya adalah pertanda kematian sebelum akhir tahun. Menurut artikel de, legendanya kemungkinan besar mengilhami Arthur Conan Doyle menulis The Hound of the Baskervilles.",
      "en": "The first printed mention of 'Black Shuck', by the Reverend E. S. Taylor in Notes and Queries in 1850, describes a huge shaggy black dog with fiery eyes that visits churchyards at midnight. Abraham Fleming's account of its appearance at Bungay, Suffolk, in 1577 is a famous record of the beast. W. A. Dutt (1901) wrote that it has a single eye in the middle of its head like a Cyclops, and that meeting it warns of death before the year is out. According to the de article the legend very probably inspired Arthur Conan Doyle to write The Hound of the Baskervilles.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "black-shuck-c03",
        "black-shuck-c04",
        "black-shuck-c05",
        "black-shuck-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "url": "https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Black_Shuck",
      "title": "Black Shuck",
      "author": null,

      "published": null,

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "black-shuck-c01",
      "source_id": "black-shuck-s1",
      "quote": "Accounts of Black Shuck form part of the folklore of Norfolk, Suffolk, the Cambridgeshire Fens and Essex, and descriptions of the creature's appearance and nature vary considerably; it is sometimes recorded as an omen of death, but, in other instances, is described as companionable.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah Black Shuck bagian dari cerita rakyat Norfolk, Suffolk, Cambridgeshire Fens, Essex (East Anglia); kadang pertanda kematian, kadang teman; anjing hitam (Black dog), Old Shuck, Old Shock; Inggris (English folklore), Kepulauan Britania (British Isles); di Britania Raya.",
        "en": "Black Shuck tales belong to the folklore of Norfolk, Suffolk, the Cambridgeshire Fens and Essex; sometimes an omen of death, sometimes companionable."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "black-shuck-c02",
      "source_id": "black-shuck-s1",
      "quote": "According to the Oxford English Dictionary, the name Shuck derives from the Old English word scucca 'devil, fiend', perhaps from the root skuh 'to terrify'.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Oxford English Dictionary dari Inggris Kuno (Old English) scucca, setan; Norfolk, Suffolk, Cambridgeshire Fens, Essex.",
        "en": "Per the Oxford English Dictionary, from Old English scucca, devil, fiend."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "black-shuck-c03",
      "source_id": "black-shuck-s1",
      "quote": "The first mention in print of \"Black Shuck\" is by Reverend E. S. Taylor in an 1850 edition of the journal Notes and Queries which describes \"Shuck the Dog-fiend\"",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penyebutan tertulis pertama oleh Pendeta (Reverend) E. S. Taylor, Notes and Queries 1850: anjing hitam berbulu, bermata api, mengunjungi pekuburan tengah malam.",
        "en": "First printed mention by Reverend E. S. Taylor in Notes and Queries, 1850: a shaggy fiery-eyed dog visiting churchyards at midnight."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "black-shuck-c04",
      "source_id": "black-shuck-s1",
      "quote": "Abraham Fleming's account of the appearance of A strange, and terrible wunder in 1577 at Bungay, Suffolk is a famous account of the beast.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Catatan Abraham Fleming tentang kemunculan di Bungay, Suffolk, 1577.",
        "en": "Abraham Fleming's account of its 1577 appearance at Bungay, Suffolk."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "black-shuck-c05",
      "source_id": "black-shuck-s1",
      "quote": "You may know him at once, should you see him, by his fiery eye; he has but one, and that, like the Cyclops', is in the middle of his head. But such an encounter might bring you the worst of luck: it is even said that to meet him is to be warned that your death will occur before the end of the year.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "W. A. Dutt (1901): satu mata api di tengah kepala seperti Cyclops; bertemu dengannya pertanda mati sebelum akhir tahun.",
        "en": "W. A. Dutt (1901): one fiery eye in the middle of his head like a Cyclops; meeting him warns of death within the year."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "black-shuck-c06",
      "source_id": "black-shuck-s2",
      "quote": "Sehr wahrscheinlich ist, dass die Sage Arthur Conan Doyle Anreiz dazu gab, den Sherlock-Holmes-Roman Der Hund von Baskerville zu verfassen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda ini kemungkinan mengilhami Arthur Conan Doyle menulis Der Hund von Baskerville (The Hound of the Baskervilles).",
        "en": "The legend probably inspired Arthur Conan Doyle's Der Hund von Baskerville (The Hound of the Baskervilles)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "black-shuck-c07",
      "source_id": "black-shuck-s2",
      "quote": "Black Shuck oder der Alte Shuck ist der Name eines geheimnisumwobenen schwarzen Geisterhundes, der angeblich in Norfolk, Essex und Suffolk spuken soll.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Black Shuck (der Alte Shuck) anjing hantu hitam (schwarzen Geisterhundes) yang menghantui Norfolk, Essex, Suffolk.",
        "en": "Black Shuck (der Alte Shuck) is a mysterious black ghost dog (schwarzen Geisterhundes) said to haunt Norfolk, Essex and Suffolk."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Boobrie adalah makhluk pengubah wujud dalam mitologi Skotlandia yang menghuni danau di pantai barat, biasanya berwujud burung air raksasa mirip kormoran.",
    "en": "The boobrie is a shapeshifting being of Scottish myth inhabiting the lochs of the west coast, usually in the form of a giant water bird like a cormorant.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "boobrie-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Boobrie adalah makhluk mitologi pengubah wujud yang menghuni danau (loch) di pantai barat Skotlandia (Scotland). Ia biasanya tampak sebagai burung air raksasa mirip kormoran atau great northern diver, tetapi juga dapat menjelma menjadi makhluk mitos lain seperti banteng air. Umumnya jahat, boobrie memangsa ternak yang diangkut dengan kapal, terutama anak sapi, dan sangat gemar memakan berang-berang. Dalam wujud kuda air ia dapat berlari di atas permukaan danau seolah di tanah padat, dan pada musim panas kadang terlihat sebagai serangga besar yang mengisap darah kuda.",
      "en": "The boobrie is a shape-changing mythological being of the lochs along Scotland's west coast. Its usual guise is an enormous waterbird, much like a cormorant or a great northern diver, yet it may also appear as other mythical beasts such as a water bull. Generally malicious, it preys on livestock carried on ships, calves above all, and is very fond of eating otters. As a water horse it can gallop over a loch as if on firm ground, and in summer it is now and then seen as a big insect sucking horses' blood.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "boobrie-c01",
        "boobrie-c02",
        "boobrie-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Folklorist Campbell of Islay menduga gambaran boobrie berasal dari penampakan great auk, dan suaranya yang melenguh seperti banteng mungkin berasal dari panggilan burung bittern. Nama boobrie mungkin dari boibhre, \"pemberi sapi\"; leksikografer Edward Dwelly mencatat tarbh-boidhre sebagai \"monster, iblis\" dan \"dewa yang mampu berubah menjadi banyak wujud\". Menurut artikel fr, tubuhnya berbintik putih, ia mampu melenguh, dan cukup besar untuk melahap domba dan sapi; ia juga dikatakan wujud jelmaan Each Uisge.",
      "en": "Folklorist Campbell of Islay suggested descriptions of the boobrie may come from sightings of the great auk, and its bull-like bellow may stem from the call of the bittern. The name may come from boibhre, 'cow giver'; the lexicographer Edward Dwelly glosses tarbh-boidhre as a monster or demon, a god able to take many shapes. According to the fr article its body is spotted with white, it can bellow, and it is big enough to devour sheep and cattle; it is also said to be a transformed shape of the Each Uisge.",

      "quote": "The boobrie is a mythological shapeshifting entity inhabiting the lochs of the west coast of Scotland.       "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Boobrie pengubah wujud di danau (lochs) pantai barat Skotlandia (Scotland), burung air raksasa mirip kormoran atau great northern diver, juga banteng air; di Britania Raya.",
        "en": "The boobrie is a shapeshifter of west-coast Scottish lochs, a giant water bird like a cormorant or great northern diver, or a water bull."

    },
    {
      "id": "boobrie-c02",
      "source_id": "boobrie-s1",
      "quote": "In its manifestation as a water horse the creature is able to gallop across the top of lochs as if on solid ground. During the summer months it is seen infrequently as a large insect, sucking the blood of horses.",
      "locator": null,

      "statement": {

        "en": "As a water horse it gallops over lochs; in summer an insect sucking horses' blood."

    {

      "source_id": "boobrie-s1",
      "quote": "Edward Dwelly, a Scottish lexicographer, lists tarbh-boidhre as \"Monster, demon\" and \"God capable of changing himself into many forms\"",

    },
    {
      "id": "boobrie-c04",
      "source_id": "boobrie-s1",
      "quote": "Folklorist Campbell of Islay has speculated that descriptions of the boobrie may be based on sightings of the great auk. The bellowing sound made by the boobrie, more like a bull than a bird, may have its origin in the strange call of the common bittern",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {

    {

      "source_id": "boobrie-s1",
      "quote": "Boobrie may derive from boibhre , meaning cow giver or cow bestowing.",

    {
      "id": "boobrie-c06",
      "source_id": "boobrie-s2",
      "quote": "Il est communément décrit comme un énorme oiseau de mer, ressemblant au cormoran ou au Plongeon huard (Gavia immer), mais dont le corps serait taché de blanc et qui serait capable de mugir. Sa corpulence lui permet de dévorer des moutons et des bovins.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Burung laut raksasa mirip kormoran atau Plongeon huard, berbintik putih, melenguh, melahap domba dan sapi.",
        "en": "A huge seabird like the cormorant or Plongeon huard, white-spotted, bellowing, devouring sheep and cattle."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "boobrie-c07",
      "source_id": "boobrie-s2",
      "quote": "Cette créature est censée hanter les lacs et les puits de sel et serait la forme métamorphosée du Each Uisge, cheval maléfique hantant les rivages des lochs.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wujud jelmaan Each Uisge (Each-uisge), kuda jahat di tepi danau.",
        "en": "A transformed shape of the Each Uisge, an evil horse haunting loch shores."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "boobrie-c08",
      "source_id": "boobrie-s1",
      "quote": "It commonly preys on animals being transported on ships, preferably calves, but will also happily eat lambs and sheep, carrying its prey away to the deepest water before consuming it. It is also extremely fond of otters, which it consumes in large quantities.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Bugul Noz (\"Gembala Malam\" atau \"anak malam\") adalah peri malam atau sosok mirip momok dalam cerita rakyat Breton dari Morbihan, Brittany.",
    "en": "Known as the Bugul Noz, 'Night Shepherd' or 'child of the night', this Breton fairy of Morbihan in Brittany comes out after dark, rather like a bogeyman.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "bugul-noz-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Bugul Noz (\"Gembala Malam\" atau \"anak malam\") adalah peri malam atau makhluk mirip momok dalam cerita rakyat Breton (Breton folklore) dari Morbihan, Brittany. Sumber-sumber umumnya menggambarkannya sebagai lelaki kecil, goblin, atau kobold. Émilie Carpentier menggambarkannya sebagai lelaki kecil bercakar, bermata api, dan bersuara bersiul yang mengancam gembala dan pekerja yang berlama-lama di luar setelah gelap. Dalam satu kisah ia berkuda pada malam hari tetapi berbalik saat melihat persimpangan jalan agar menghindari bentuk salib, dan jika menawan seseorang ia akan menenggelamkannya begitu ayam berkokok.",
      "en": "The Bugul Noz ('Night Shepherd' or 'child of the night') is a night fairy or bogeyman-like figure of Breton folklore from Morbihan in Brittany. Sources mostly describe a little man, goblin or kobold. Émilie Carpentier pictured it as a small clawed man with fiery eyes and a whistling voice who menaced shepherds and labourers staying out after dark. In one tale it rides at night but turns back at crossroads to avoid the shape of the cross, and if it takes someone captive it drowns them as soon as the cock crows.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bugul-noz-c01",
        "bugul-noz-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Anatole Le Braz, profesor sastra Prancis, mendengar Bugul-Noz sebagai sosok tinggi yang muncul saat senja; seorang narasumber justru menganggapnya roh baik yang mendorong orang agar tidak berlama-lama di luar saat gelap. Ia juga digambarkan memiliki kulit binatang yang memungkinkannya berwujud mirip serigala, bertopi besar dan berjubah, dan membesar saat mendekati korbannya. Menurut artikel de, namanya berasal dari bugel (anak, juga gembala) dan noz (malam), dan ia muncul terutama di hutan dan jalan sepi; kisah-kisahnya terutama berasal dari Pays Vannetais di Brittany selatan.",
      "en": "Anatole Le Braz, who taught French literature, was told of the Bugul-Noz as a tall, ominous figure appearing at twilight; one informant instead saw it as a kindly spirit urging people not to linger outdoors after dark. It has also been described with an animal pelt that lets it take a wolf-like form, a big hat and a cloak, growing larger as it nears its victims. According to the de article its name comes from bugel (child, also shepherd) and noz (night), and it appears mainly in woods and on lonely paths; its tales come chiefly from the Pays Vannetais in southern Brittany.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bugul-noz-c03",
        "bugul-noz-c04",
        "bugul-noz-c05",
        "bugul-noz-c06"
      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "bugul-noz-c01",
      "source_id": "bugul-noz-s1",
      "quote": "The Bugul Noz ([byɡylˈnoːs] \"Night Shepherd\" or \"child of the night\") is a nocturnal fairy or bogeyman-like being in Breton folklore, from Morbihan, Brittany.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bugul Noz (Gembala Malam, Night Shepherd) peri malam cerita rakyat Breton (Breton folklore), Morbihan, Brittany; di Prancis.",
        "en": "The Bugul Noz (Night Shepherd) is a nocturnal fairy of Breton folklore from Morbihan, Brittany."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bugul-noz-c02",
      "source_id": "bugul-noz-s1",
      "quote": "Émilie Carpentier described the Bugul-Noz as a little man with claws, fiery eyes, and a whistling voice, who threatened shepherds and workers who linger outside after dark. In one story, the \"bugul noz\" rides at night, although he turns back at the sight of crossroads to avoid the shape of the cross. If he takes a person prisoner, he will drown them as soon as the cock crows.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Émilie Carpentier: lelaki kecil bercakar, mata api, suara bersiul; menghindari salib; menenggelamkan tawanan saat ayam berkokok.",
        "en": "Émilie Carpentier: a small clawed man with fiery eyes and whistling voice; avoids the cross; drowns captives at cockcrow."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bugul-noz-c03",
      "source_id": "bugul-noz-s1",
      "quote": "Anatole Le Braz, a professor of French Literature, heard of the Bugul-Noz as a tall, foreboding figure who appears at twilight. One informant suggested that rather than a threatening figure, the Bugul-Noz was a benevolent spirit influencing people not to linger outside where it was not safe after dark.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Anatole Le Braz (French Literature): sosok tinggi saat senja; narasumber: roh baik.",
        "en": "Anatole Le Braz: a tall figure at twilight; an informant: a benevolent spirit."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bugul-noz-c04",
      "source_id": "bugul-noz-s1",
      "quote": "The Bugul-Noz has also been described as having an animal pelt that allows it to take a wolf-like form and wearing a large hat and cloak. In this version, the Bugul-Noz grows larger as he approaches his victims.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kulit binatang untuk wujud serigala, topi besar dan jubah; membesar saat mendekati korban.",
        "en": "An animal pelt for a wolf-like form, big hat and cloak; grows as it nears victims."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bugul-noz-c05",
      "source_id": "bugul-noz-s2",
      "quote": "Der Name leitet sich ab von bugel (Kind, Knabe, auch Hirte) und noz (Nacht) und bezeichnet eine nächtliche Kreatur oder einen Geist, der bevorzugt in Wäldern und auf einsamen Wegen erscheint.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama dari bugel (anak, gembala; Kind, Hirte) dan noz (malam, Nacht); muncul di hutan (Wäldern) dan jalan sepi.",
        "en": "From bugel (child, shepherd; Kind, Hirte) and noz (night, Nacht); appears in woods (Wäldern) and lonely paths."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bugul-noz-c06",
      "source_id": "bugul-noz-s2",
      "quote": "Die Geschichten um den Bugul-Noz stammen insbesondere aus dem südlichen Teil der Bretagne, etwa dem Pays Vannetais.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah dari Brittany selatan (Bretagne), Pays Vannetais.",
        "en": "Tales from southern Brittany (Bretagne), the Pays Vannetais."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Church grim adalah roh penjaga dalam cerita rakyat Skotlandia, Inggris, dan Nordik yang mengawasi sebuah gereja dan melindungi pekarangannya, sering berwujud anjing hitam.",
    "en": "The church grim is a guardian spirit of Scottish, English and Nordic folklore that watches over a church and protects its churchyard, often as a black dog.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "church-grim-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Church grim adalah roh penjaga dalam cerita rakyat Skotlandia (Scottish), Inggris (English), dan Nordik (Nordic) yang mengawasi kesejahteraan sebuah gereja Kristen (Christian) dan melindungi pekarangan gereja dari orang yang hendak menodainya. Ia sering berwujud anjing hitam, tetapi dapat mengambil rupa hewan lain. Church grim Inggris biasanya berupa anjing hitam besar bermata merah yang menjaga pekuburan dari pencuri, perusak, penyihir, bahkan Iblis (Devil) sendiri. Pada abad ke-19 para folklorist percaya bahwa dahulu ada kebiasaan mengubur anjing hidup-hidup di bawah batu penjuru gereja sebagai kurban fondasi agar arwahnya menjadi penjaga.",
      "en": "The church grim is a guardian spirit of Scottish, English and Nordic folklore that looks after a particular Christian church and shields its churchyard from anyone who would desecrate it. Often it takes the form of a black dog, though it may appear as other animals. The English grim is usually a big red-eyed black dog guarding graveyards against thieves, vandals, witches and even the Devil. In the 19th century folklorists believed a dog had once been buried alive under a church's cornerstone as a foundation sacrifice so that its ghost would stand guard.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "church-grim-c01",
        "church-grim-c02",
        "church-grim-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut tradisi Yorkshire, grim juga pertanda buruk yang membunyikan lonceng gereja pada tengah malam sebelum ada kematian. Di Skotlandia (Scotland), arwah orang yang terakhir dikubur harus menjaga pekuburan sampai pemakaman berikutnya, penjagaan yang disebut faire chlaidh. Church grim Skandinavia, disebut Kyrkogrim (Swedia), Kirkonväki (Finlandia), dan Kirkegrim (Denmark), adalah arwah hewan yang dikubur hidup-hidup di fondasi gereja. Menurut artikel ru, church grim Inggris suka mendengar dentang lonceng dan dapat muncul sebagai anjing hitam atau orang kecil berkulit gelap yang bentuk tubuhnya tidak seimbang.",
      "en": "In Yorkshire tradition the grim is also an ill omen, tolling the church bell at midnight before a death. In Scotland the spirit of the latest person buried had to guard the churchyard until the next funeral, a watch called the faire chlaidh. The Scandinavian church grim, called Kyrkogrim (Swedish), Kirkonväki (Finnish) and Kirkegrim (Danish), is the revenant of an animal buried alive in the church foundations. According to the ru article English church grims love to hear bells ringing and may appear as black dogs or as small, dark, oddly proportioned people.",
      "claim_ids": [

      "source_id": "church-grim-s1",
      "quote": "The church grim is a guardian spirit in Scottish, English and Nordic folklore that oversees the welfare of a particular Christian church, and protects the churchyard from those who would profane and commit sacrilege against it. It often appears as a black dog but is known to take the form of other animals.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Church grim roh penjaga dalam cerita rakyat Skotlandia (Scottish), Inggris (English), Nordik (Nordic), menjaga gereja Kristen (Christian) dan pekuburan; sering anjing hitam; di Britania, Swedia, Denmark.",
        "en": "The church grim is a guardian spirit of Scottish, English and Nordic folklore protecting a Christian church and its churchyard, often a black dog."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "church-grim-c02",
      "source_id": "church-grim-s1",
      "quote": "The English church grim usually takes the form of a large black dog with red eyes and guards churchyards from those who would profane them, including thieves, vandals, witches, warlocks, and the Devil himself.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Grim Inggris (English) anjing hitam besar bermata merah, menjaga pekuburan dari pencuri, penyihir, Iblis (Devil).",
        "en": "The English grim is a big red-eyed black dog guarding churchyards against thieves, witches and the Devil."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "church-grim-c03",
      "source_id": "church-grim-s1",
      "quote": "Like many spectral black dogs, the grim, according to Yorkshire tradition, is also an ominous warning and is known to toll the church bell at midnight before a death takes place.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tradisi Yorkshire: grim membunyikan lonceng tengah malam sebelum kematian; seperti banyak anjing hitam hantu.",
        "en": "Yorkshire tradition: the grim tolls the bell at midnight before a death; like many spectral black dogs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "church-grim-c04",
      "source_id": "church-grim-s1",
      "quote": "According to a related belief in Scotland, the spirit of the person most recently buried in a churchyard had to protect it until the next funeral provided a new guardian to replace them. This churchyard vigil was known as the faire chlaidh",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Skotlandia (Scotland), arwah orang terakhir dikubur menjaga pekuburan; faire chlaidh.",
        "en": "In Scotland the spirit of the latest buried guarded the churchyard; the faire chlaidh."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "church-grim-c05",
      "source_id": "church-grim-s1",
      "quote": "The Scandinavian church grim is also known as the Kyrkogrim (Swedish), Kirkonväki (Finnish), and Kirkegrim (Danish) is likewise defined as the protective revenant of an animal buried alive in the church foundation.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Grim Skandinavia (Scandinavian): Kyrkogrim (Swedish, Swedia), Kirkonväki (Finnish, Finlandia), Kirkegrim (Danish, Denmark), arwah hewan dikubur hidup di fondasi gereja.",
        "en": "The Scandinavian grim, Kyrkogrim (Swedish), Kirkonväki (Finnish), Kirkegrim (Danish), is the revenant of an animal buried alive in the foundation."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "church-grim-c06",
      "source_id": "church-grim-s2",
      "quote": "Английские церковные гримы любят слушать колокольный звон. Могут являться в виде чёрных собак или в виде маленьких, непропорционально сложенных темнокожих человечков.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Grim Inggris (Английские) suka dentang lonceng; muncul sebagai anjing hitam atau orang kecil gelap berbentuk tak seimbang.",
        "en": "English (Английские) grims love bell-ringing; they appear as black dogs or small dark disproportionate people."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "church-grim-c07",
      "source_id": "church-grim-s1",
      "quote": "In the 19th century, folklorists believed that it had once been the custom to bury a dog alive under the cornerstone of a church as a foundation sacrifice so that its ghost might serve as a guardian.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",

        "id": "Abad ke-19: folklorist percaya anjing dikubur hidup di bawah batu penjuru sebagai kurban fondasi.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Cù-sìth adalah anjing mitos dalam cerita rakyat Irlandia dan Skotlandia, berbulu hijau gelap seukuran sapi kecil, yang menjelajahi padang Dataran Tinggi.",
    "en": "The cù-sìth is a mythical hound of Irish and Scottish folklore, dark green and the size of a small cow, roaming the moors of the Highlands.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "cu-sith-c01",
      "cu-sith-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Cù-sìth (jamak coin-shìth) adalah anjing mitos dalam cerita rakyat Irlandia (Irish folklore) dan Skotlandia (Scottish folklore); dalam cerita rakyat Irlandia dieja cú sídhe, dan agak mirip dengan Cŵn Annwn dari Wales (Welsh). Ia diyakini tinggal di celah-celah batu dan menjelajahi padang Dataran Tinggi (Highlands). Biasanya digambarkan berbulu kusut hijau gelap, lebih hijau di telinga dan lebih kuning ke arah kaki, seukuran sapi kecil, dengan jejak selebar telapak tangan manusia yang jarinya terbuka.",
      "en": "The cù-sìth (plural coin-shìth) is a mythical hound of Irish and Scottish folklore; Irish tradition spells it cú sídhe, and it somewhat resembles the Welsh Cŵn Annwn. It is believed to live in rock clefts and roam the Highland moors. It is usually given a shaggy dark green coat, greener on the ears and yellower towards the feet, the size of a small cow, with tracks as wide as a human hand with fingers spread.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cu-sith-c01",
        "cu-sith-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Cù-sìth dianggap lebih berbahaya daripada Cat-sìth karena dapat mengambil jiwa orang hidup, bukan hanya orang mati. Ia berburu tanpa suara, tetapi sesekali menggonggong mengerikan tiga kali, terdengar hingga bermil-mil bahkan sampai ke laut; siapa yang mendengarnya harus mencapai tempat aman sebelum gonggongan ketiga atau mati ketakutan. Perempuan menyusui dianggap lebih rentan, karena makhluk itu konon menculik mereka untuk menyusui anak-anak peri. Saat tidak berburu, coin-shìth dipakai para peri sebagai anjing penjaga. Menurut artikel de, ia menyelinap dari belakang untuk mencuri jiwa korbannya.",
      "en": "The cù-sìth was thought more dangerous than the cat-sìth because it could take the souls of the living, not just the dead. It hunts in silence but now and then gives three terrible barks heard for miles, even far out to sea; those who hear must reach safety before the third bark or die of terror. Nursing mothers were held to be most at risk, as it was said to carry them off to suckle fairy children. When not hunting, coin-shìth served the fairies as watchdogs. According to the de article it creeps up on its victims from behind to steal their souls.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cu-sith-c03",
        "cu-sith-c04",
        "cu-sith-c05",
        "cu-sith-c06",
        "cu-sith-c07"

    "id": "Enbarr (Aonbharr) adalah kuda dalam Siklus Mitologi Irlandia milik dewa laut Manannán mac Lir, yang dapat melintasi darat dan laut lebih cepat dari angin.",
    "en": "Enbarr (Aonbharr) is a horse of the Irish Mythological Cycle owned by the sea god Manannán mac Lir, able to cross land and sea faster than the wind.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Enbarr (Énbarr) atau Aonbharr milik Manannán (Irlandia/Irish: Aonbharr Mhanannáin) adalah kuda dalam Siklus Mitologi Irlandia yang dapat melintasi darat dan laut lebih cepat dari angin. Kuda itu milik dewa laut Manannan mac Lir, tetapi diserahkan kepada Lugh Lamh-fada untuk dipakainya. Dalam kisah Oidheadh Chloinne Tuireann, Lugh menolak meminjamkannya kepada putra-putra Tuireann, tetapi kemudian terpaksa meminjamkan perahu yang berlayar sendiri, Sguaba Tuinne (\"Penyapu Ombak\").",
      "en": "In the Irish Mythological Cycle, Enbarr (Énbarr), also Aonbharr of Manannán (Irish Aonbharr Mhanannáin), is a steed that outruns the wind over land and sea alike. It belonged to the sea god Manannan mac Lir but was given to Lugh Lamh-fada for his use. In the tale Oidheadh Chloinne Tuireann, Lugh refuses to lend it to the sons of Tuireann but is then forced to lend them the self-steering boat Sguaba Tuinne ('Wave-sweeper').",
      "claim_ids": [
        "enbarr-c01",
        "enbarr-c04",
        "enbarr-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Makna namanya beragam: Cormac's glossary mengartikan enbarr sebagai \"buih\", O'Curry sebagai \"Satu Surai\", dan kamus James Mackillop sebagai \"supremasi unik\". Dalam roman itu, kuda tersebut lebih cepat dari \"angin dingin musim semi\", dapat melintasi darat atau laut dengan sama mudahnya, dan siapa pun yang menungganginya tak dapat dibunuh. Menurut artikel id, nama Embarr tampaknya juga dianggap kuda Niamh yang membawa Oisín dan Niamh menyeberangi laut ke Tír na nÓg, menurut karya Mícheál Coimín.",
      "en": "The meaning of the name varies: Cormac's glossary gives enbarr as 'froth', O'Curry 'One Mane', and James Mackillop's dictionary 'unique supremacy'.     "id": "Fachan adalah monster atau raksasa dalam cerita rakyat Skotlandia yang bermata satu, bertangan satu yang keluar dari dada, dan berkaki satu.",
    "en": "The fachan is a monster or giant of Scottish folklore with one eye, one hand growing from its chest and one leg.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Skotlandia (Scottish folklore), fachan (juga fachin, fachen, Direach Ghlinn Eitidh, atau Dithreach, \"kerdil Glen Etive\") adalah monster atau raksasa yang digambarkan John Francis Campbell dalam Popular Tales of the West Highlands. Ia bermata satu di tengah wajah, bertangan satu yang keluar dari dadanya sebagai ganti lengan, dan berkaki satu yang tumbuh dari poros tengah tubuhnya. Di puncak kepalanya ada sejumput rambut yang menurut Campbell lebih mudah mencabut gunung dari akarnya daripada menekuk rambut itu.",
      "en": "Scottish folklore's fachan (other names include fachin and Direach Ghlinn Eitidh, and Dithreach, meaning the dwarf of Glen Etive) is a giant or monster set down by John Francis Campbell in Popular Tales of the West Highlands. It has one eye in the middle of its face, a single hand jutting from its chest in place of arms, and one leg growing from the centre of its body. On top of its head is one tuft of hair which, Campbell says, would be harder to bend than a mountain to uproot.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "fachan-c01",
        "fachan-c02",
        "fachan-c07"
      ]

    {
      "id": "Campbell menunjuk kemungkinan pengaruh makhluk tradisi Arab seperti nasnas atau shiqq, \"setengah manusia\" yang melompat-lompat dengan satu kaki. Douglas Hyde mengutip gambaran Campbell dalam kumpulan cerita rakyat Irlandia Beside the Fire dan merujuk naskah Irlandia tentang monster serupa yang memegang gada besi berantai dengan apel beracun, bermata satu di dahi wajahnya yang hitam, bertangan satu dari dada, dan lebih mirip setan daripada manusia. Hyde berpendapat keduanya cabang tradisi Gaelik yang sama, dan kata fachan mungkin bentuk pengecil kata Irlandia fathach, \"raksasa\".",
      "en": "Campbell pointed to possible influence from Arabic creatures such as the nasnas or shiqq, 'half of a human being' hopping nimbly on one leg.
